bore\n\n    # Add grip features: two opposing lobes on the sides\n    # This ensures the grip measurements are met (28mm min, 12% wider at max reach)\n    lobe_height = knob_height / 2.0  # At mid-height\n    lobe_radius = 8.0  # Lobe extends outward\n    lobe_width = 10.0\n\n    lobe1 = cylinder(r=lobe_radius, h=lobe_width)\n    lobe1 = lobe1.right(knob_diameter/2.0 + lobe_radius)\n    lobe1 = lobe1.up(lobe_height)\n\n    lobe2 = cylinder(r=lobe_radius, h=lobe_width)\n    lobe2 = lobe2.left(knob_diameter/2.0 + lobe_radius)\n    lobe2 = lobe2.up(lobe_height)\n\n    knob = knob.add(lobe1, lobe2)\n\n    # Add a small chamfer to the bottom for bed contact and to meet printability\n    knob = knob.chamfer(edges(knob).select(lambda f: f.z.min < 1), 0.5)\n\n    # Polish the outer edges for a finished look\n    knob = knob.polish()\n\n    return knob","new_string":"from nurb import *\nimport math\n\ndef valve_knob(shaft_diameter=8.0, shaft_across_flat=6.5, knob_diameter=42.0, knob_height=18.0):\n    \"\"\"Replacement knob for a D-shaft valve.\n\n    shaft_diameter: diameter of the circular side of the D-stem\n    shaft_across_flat: width of the flat side of the D-stem\n    knob_diameter: outer diameter of the knob body\n    knob_height: height of the knob above the stem\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Bore dimensions with clearance for 0.3mm grown stem\n    bore_clearance = 0.3\n    bore_diameter = shaft_diameter + 2 * bore_clearance\n    bore_across_flat = shaft_across_flat + 2 * bore_clearance\n    bore_depth = 15.0  # Deep enough for stem (12.0) with margin\n\n    # The D-shaft profile: circular on one side, flat on the other\n    # bore_radius is the radius of the circular side\n    bore_radius = bore_diameter / 2.0\n    # flat_cutoff is how deep the flat cuts into the bore\n    flat_cutoff = bore_diameter - bore_across_flat\n    # flat_y is the Y coordinate of the flat from the center\n    flat_y = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create the knob body as a cylinder\n    knob_body = Cylinder(radius=knob_diameter/2.0, height=knob_height)\n\n    # Create a D-shaped bore using a circle and subtracting a box for the flat\n    # First, create a cylinder for the bore\n    bore_cyl = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n\n    # Create a box to cut away the flat side\n    # The box should be positioned to remove the flat portion\n    flat_box_height = bore_diameter  # tall enough to extend through the bore\n    flat_box_width = bore_diameter * 2  # wide enough to fully cut through\n    flat_box_depth = (bore_radius - flat_y)  # from the flat line to the bottom\n\n    with BuildSketch() as sketch:\n        with Locations(Plane.XZ):\n            # Draw a circle for the round part\n            Circle(bore_radius)\n\n    bore_2d = make_face(sketch)\n\n    # Create the D-shaped profile by cutting the flat\n    with BuildSketch() as d_sketch:\n        with Locations(Plane.XZ):\n            # Draw a semicircle on top (the round part)\n            for angle in range(0, 181):\n                rad = math.radians(angle)\n                x = bore_radius * math.cos(rad)\n                y = bore_radius * math.sin(rad)\n                if angle == 0:\n                    d_sketch.move(x, y)\n                else:\n                    d_sketch.line((bore_radius * math.cos(math.radians(angle)),\n                                   bore_radius * math.sin(math.radians(angle))))\n            # Draw the flat line\n            d_sketch.line((bore_radius, flat_y))\n            d_sketch.line((-bore_radius, flat_y))\n            # Close the shape\n            d_sketch.close()\n\n    # Extrude the D-shape to create the bore\n    bore = extrude(d_sketch.sketch, amount=bore_depth)\n\n    # Position the bore at the top of the knob\n    bore = bore.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2.0))\n\n    # Create the final knob by subtracting the bore\n    knob = knob_body - bore\n\n    # Add grip features: two opposing lobes to meet grip requirements\n    # At mid-height of the knob\n    lobe_height = knob_height / 2.0\n    lobe_extension = 8.0  # extends beyond the knob diameter\n\n    # Create two cylindrical lobes on opposite sides\n    lobe_radius = 5.0\n    lobe_pos = knob_diameter / 2.0 + lobe_radius\n\n    lobe1 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=10.0)\n    lobe1 = lobe1.translate((lobe_pos, 0, lobe_height))\n\n    lobe2 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=10.0)\n    lobe2 = lobe2.translate((-lobe_pos, 0, lobe_height))\n\n    knob = knob + lobe1 + lobe2\n\n    # Chamfer the bottom edges for bed contact\n    bottom_edges = [e for e in knob.edges if abs(e.center.z) < 1.0]\n    if bottom_edges:\n        knob = chamfer(bottom_edges, 0.5)\n\n    # Polish the outer edges\n    visible_edges = concave_edges(knob)\n    if visible_edges:\n        knob = polish(knob, visible_edges, 1.0)\n    else:\n        knob = polish(knob, knob.edges, 1.0)\n\n    return knob"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":900,"cache_read_input_tokens":33463,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":900},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"96d873a3-6752-4b58-b83d-6cc95b981bcc","uuid":"89ccc97f-a376-4120-a731-b54626f0813b","timestamp":"2026-08-24T01:28:42.777Z","request_id":"req_011CeLhsXPsp7JMegyn96Ur9"}

bore_across_flat\n    # flat_y is the Y coordinate of the flat from the center\n    flat_y = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create the knob body as a cylinder\n    knob_body = Cylinder(radius=knob_diameter/2.0, height=knob_height)\n\n    # Create a D-shaped bore using a circle and subtracting a box for the flat\n    # First, create a cylinder for the bore\n    bore_cyl = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n\n    # Create a box to cut away the flat side\n    # The box should be positioned to remove the flat portion\n    flat_box_height = bore_diameter  # tall enough to extend through the bore\n    flat_box_width = bore_diameter * 2  # wide enough to fully cut through\n    flat_box_depth = (bore_radius - flat_y)  # from the flat line to the bottom\n\n    with BuildSketch() as sketch:\n        with Locations(Plane.XZ):\n            # Draw a circle for the round part\n            Circle(bore_radius)\n\n    bore_2d = make_face(sketch)\n\n    # Create the D-shaped profile by cutting the flat\n    with BuildSketch() as d_sketch:\n        with Locations(Plane.XZ):\n            # Draw a semicircle on top (the round part)\n            for angle in range(0, 181):\n                rad = math.radians(angle)\n                x = bore_radius * math.cos(rad)\n                y = bore_radius * math.sin(rad)\n                if angle == 0:\n                    d_sketch.move(x, y)\n                else:\n                    d_sketch.line((bore_radius * math.cos(math.radians(angle)),\n                                   bore_radius * math.sin(math.radians(angle))))\n            # Draw the flat line\n            d_sketch.line((bore_radius, flat_y))\n            d_sketch.line((-bore_radius, flat_y))\n            # Close the shape\n            d_sketch.close()\n\n    # Extrude the D-shape to create the bore\n    bore = extrude(d_sketch.sketch, amount=bore_depth)\n\n    # Position the bore at the top of the knob\n    bore = bore.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth/2.0))\n\n    # Create the final knob by subtracting the bore\n    knob = knob_body - bore\n\n    # Add grip features: two opposing lobes to meet grip requirements\n    # At mid-height of the knob\n    lobe_height = knob_height / 2.0\n    lobe_extension = 8.0  # extends beyond the knob diameter\n\n    # Create two cylindrical lobes on opposite sides\n    lobe_radius = 5.0\n    lobe_pos = knob_diameter / 2.0 + lobe_radius\n\n    lobe1 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=10.0)\n    lobe1 = lobe1.translate((lobe_pos, 0, lobe_height))\n\n    lobe2 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=10.0)\n    lobe2 = lobe2.translate((-lobe_pos, 0, lobe_height))\n\n    knob = knob + lobe1 + lobe2\n\n    # Chamfer the bottom edges for bed contact\n    bottom_edges = [e for e in knob.edges if abs(e.center.z) < 1.0]\n    if bottom_edges:\n        knob = chamfer(bottom_edges, 0.5)\n\n    # Polish the outer edges\n    visible_edges = concave_edges(knob)\n    if visible_edges:\n        knob = polish(knob, visible_edges, 1.0)\n    else:\n        knob = polish(knob, knob.edges, 1.0)\n\n    return knob","new_string":"from nurb import *\n\n@part\ndef valve_knob(shaft_diameter=8.0, shaft_across_flat=6.5, knob_diameter=42.0, knob_height=18.0):\n    \"\"\"Replacement knob for a D-shaft valve.\n\n    shaft_diameter: diameter of the circular side of the D-stem\n    shaft_across_flat: width of the flat side of the D-stem\n    knob_diameter: outer diameter of the knob body\n    knob_height: height of the knob above the stem\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Bore dimensions with clearance for 0.3mm grown stem to fit, 1.0mm to jam\n    bore_clearance = 0.35  # Between 0.3 and 1.0, closer to fit\n    bore_diameter = shaft_diameter + 2 * bore_clearance\n    bore_across_flat = shaft_across_flat + 2 * bore_clearance\n    bore_depth = 14.0  # Deep enough for the stem (12.0mm) with margin\n\n    # The D-shaft geometry\n    bore_radius = bore_diameter / 2.0\n    flat_cutoff = bore_diameter - bore_across_flat\n    flat_distance_from_center = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create the main knob body as a cylinder\n    knob = Cylinder(radius=knob_diameter / 2.0, height=knob_height)\n\n    # Create the D-shaped bore\n    # Start with a cylinder for the round part\n    bore_cylinder = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n    bore_cylinder = bore_cylinder.translate((0, 0, knob_height))\n\n    # Create a box to subtract for the flat part\n    # The flat is at distance flat_distance_from_center from the center\n    flat_removal_height = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_width = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_depth = (bore_radius - flat_distance_from_center) + 1.0\n\n    flat_box = Box(\n        length=flat_removal_width,\n        width=flat_removal_height,\n        height=flat_removal_depth\n    )\n    # Position the flat box so it removes the correct part\n    flat_box = flat_box.translate((0, -flat_distance_from_center - flat_removal_depth/2.0, knob_height))\n\n    # Create the D-bore by subtracting the flat box from the cylinder\n    bore = bore_cylinder - flat_box\n\n    # Subtract the bore from the knob body\n    knob = knob - bore_across_flat\n    flat_distance_from_center = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create a cylinder for the main knob body\n    main_body = Cylinder(radius=knob_diameter / 2.0, height=knob_height)\n\n    # Create two wider cylinders to form the grip lobes - one on each side\n    # These are positioned to create a flared, easy-to-grip shape\n    lobe_radius = 7.0\n    lobe_height = knob_height / 2.0  # At mid-height\n    lobe_width = 14.0\n\n    # Lobe 1: positioned on the right side at mid-height, tilted down slightly\n    lobe1 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=lobe_width)\n    lobe1 = lobe1.rotate(Axis.Y, -30.0)  # Tilt down and out\n    lobe1 = lobe1.translate((knob_diameter / 2.0 + 4.5, 0, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Lobe 2: positioned on the left side at mid-height, tilted down slightly\n    lobe2 = Cylinder(radius=lobe_radius, height=lobe_width)\n    lobe2 = lobe2.rotate(Axis.Y, 30.0)  # Tilt down and out\n    lobe2 = lobe2.translate((-knob_diameter / 2.0 - 4.5, 0, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Combine all body parts into one solid\n    knob = main_body.fuse(lobe1).fuse(lobe2)\n\n    # Create the D-shaped bore that cuts into the top of the knob\n    bore_cylinder = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n    bore_cylinder = bore_cylinder.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2.0))\n\n    # Create a box to subtract for the flat part\n    flat_removal_height = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_width = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_depth = (bore_radius - flat_distance_from_center) + 1.0\n\n    flat_box = Box(\n        length=flat_removal_width,\n        width=flat_removal_height,\n        height=flat_removal_depth\n    )\n    flat_box = flat_box.translate((0, -flat_distance_from_center - bore_across_flat\n    flat_distance_from_center = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create a tapered cone shape: wide at mid-height for grip, smaller at top and bottom\n    # Base: wider cylinder for bed contact\n    base_diameter = 50.0\n    base_height = 3.0\n    base = Cylinder(radius=base_diameter / 2.0, height=base_height)\n    base = base.translate((0, 0, base_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Main body: cylinder at mid size\n    mid_diameter = knob_diameter\n    mid_height = knob_height - 2.0  # Leave 1mm margin from top and bottom\n    grip_width = 14.0\n    grip_depth = 3.5  # Depth of the flat cut\n\n    # Grip flat 1 (facing +X)\n    grip1 = Box(length=grip_depth + 2.0, width=grip_width, height=grip_height)\n    grip1 = grip1.translate((knob_diameter / 2.0 - 0.5, 0, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Grip flat 2 (facing -X)\n    grip2 = Box(length=grip_depth + 2.0, width=grip_width, height=grip_height)\n    grip2 = grip2.translate((-knob_diameter / 2.0 + 0.5, 0, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Subtract the grip surfaces\n    knob = knob - grip1 - grip2\n\n    # Create the D-shaped bore at the top\n    bore_cylinder = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n    bore_cylinder = bore_cylinder.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2.0))\n\n    # Create a box to subtract for the flat part of the D\n    flat_removal_height = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_width = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_depth = (bore_radius - bore_across_flat\n    flat_distance_from_center = bore_radius - flat_cutoff\n\n    # Create the main knob body as a simple cylinder\n    # This is the primary knob shape - a clean cylinder\n    knob = Cylinder(radius=knob_diameter / 2.0, height=knob_height)\n\n    # Add external grip ribs on the sides (not cut surfaces, added ribs)\n    # These ribs extend outward to provide grip without creating concave junctions\n    rib_height = knob_height - 4.0  # Ribs in the middle section\n    rib_width = 12.0\n    rib_thickness = 2.5  # How far they extend outward\n    rib_depth = 3.0\n\n    # Rib 1 on the +X side\n    rib1 = Box(length=rib_thickness, width=rib_width, height=rib_height)\n    rib1 = rib1.translate((knob_diameter / 2.0 + rib_thickness / 2.0, 0, knob_height / 2.0 + 2.0))\n\n    # Rib 2 on the -X side\n    rib2 = Box(length=rib_thickness, width=rib_width, height=rib_height)\n    rib2 = rib2.translate((-knob_diameter / 2.0 - rib_thickness / 2.0, 0, knob_height / 2.0 + 2.0))\n\n    # Add the ribs to the main body\n    knob = knob.fuse(rib1).fuse(rib2)\n\n    # Create the D-shaped bore at the top\n    bore_cylinder = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n    bore_cylinder = bore_cylinder.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2.0))\n\n    # Create a box to subtract for the flat part of the D\n    flat_removal_height = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_width = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_depth = (bore_radius - flat_distance_from_center) + 1.0\n\n    flat_box = Box(\n        length=flat_removal_width,\n        width=flat_removal_height,\n        height=flat_removal_depth\n    )\n    flat_box = flat_box.translate((0, -flat_distance_from_center - flat_removal_depth / 2.0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2.0))\n\n    # Create the D-bore\n    bore = bore_cylinder - flat_box\n\n    # Subtract the bore from the knob\n    knob = knob - bore\n\n    # Polish the outer edges for a finished look\n    knob = polish(knob, knob.edges(), 1.0)\n\n    return knob"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":8,"cache_creation_input_tokens":886,"cache_read_input_tokens":58979,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":886},"output_tokens":1,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"96d873a3-6752-4b58-b83d-6cc95b981bcc","uuid":"50bad06a-7e80-4135-8530-215745f823c0","timestamp":"2026-08-24T01:33:01.626Z","request_id":"req_011CeLiCa4tamcr743ak4h1z"}

2.0  # Leave top and bottom corners round\n\n    corner_size = corner_cut_depth * 1.5\n    offset = knob_diameter / 2.0 - corner_cut_depth / 2.0\n\n    # Create 4 corner cuts (45 degree cuts on 4 corners)\n    # Corner 1: +X, +Y\n    corner1 = Box(length=corner_size, width=corner_size, height=corner_height)\n    corner1 = corner1.translate((offset, offset, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Corner 2: +X, -Y\n    corner2 = Box(length=corner_size, width=corner_size, height=corner_height)\n    corner2 = corner2.translate((offset, -offset, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Corner 3: -X, +Y\n    corner3 = Box(length=corner_size, width=corner_size, height=corner_height)\n    corner3 = corner3.translate((-offset, offset, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Corner 4: -X, -Y\n    corner4 = Box(length=corner_size, width=corner_size, height=corner_height)\n    corner4 = corner4.translate((-offset, -offset, knob_height / 2.0))\n\n    # Subtract the corners to create octagonal profile\n    knob = knob - corner1 - corner2 - corner3 - corner4\n\n    # Create the D-shaped bore at the top\n    bore_cylinder = Cylinder(radius=bore_radius, height=bore_depth)\n    bore_cylinder = bore_cylinder.translate((0, 0, knob_height - bore_depth / 2.0))\n\n    # Create a box to subtract for the flat part of the D\n    flat_removal_height = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_width = bore_diameter * 2\n    flat_removal_depth = (bore_radius -
